\displaystyle\lvert\mathfrak{M}\odot_{G}\mathfrak{N}\rvert_{G}\leq\lvert\mathfrak{M}\rvert_{G}\lvert\mathfrak{N}\rvert_{G},

\displaystyle\quad\mathfrak{M}\odot_{g}\mathfrak{N}=a^{-2}\mathfrak{M}\odot_{G}\mathfrak{N}

(11.4b)

\displaystyle\lvert\mathfrak{S}\times_{G}\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G}\lesssim\lvert\mathfrak{S}\rvert_{G}\lvert\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G},

\displaystyle\quad(\mathfrak{S}\times\mathfrak{T})_{ij}=a^{-3}(\mathfrak{S}\times_{G}\mathfrak{T})_{ij}

(11.4c)

\displaystyle\lvert\mathfrak{S}\wedge_{G}\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G}\leq\lvert\mathfrak{S}\rvert_{G}\lvert\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G},

\displaystyle\quad\left(\mathfrak{S}\wedge\mathfrak{T}\right)_{l}=a^{-3}\left(\mathfrak{S}\wedge_{G}\mathfrak{T}\right)

(11.4d)

\displaystyle\lvert\xi\wedge_{G}\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G}\leq\lvert\xi\rvert_{G}\lvert\mathfrak{T}\rvert_{G},

\displaystyle\quad\left(\xi\wedge\mathfrak{T}\right)_{ij}=a^{-1}(\xi\wedge_{G}\mathfrak{T})_{ij}

(11.4e)

\displaystyle\lvert\text{{curl}}_{G}\mathfrak{M}\rvert_{G}\lesssim\lvert\nabla\mathfrak{M}\rvert_{G},

\displaystyle\quad\text{{curl}}\mathfrak{M}_{ij}=a^{-1}\text{{curl}}_{G}\mathfrak{M}_{ij}

Proof.

The estimates with respect to the unrescaled metric are direct consequences of the contraction identities (
11.2a
)-(
11.2c
) replacing
g
with
G
, and the scalings follow simply by tracking the effects of the rescaling in Definition
2.9
. In particular, note

(11.5)

{\bm{\varepsilon}[g]_{i}}^{cd}=g^{cj}g^{dk}\bm{\varepsilon}[g]_{ijk}=\left(a^{-2}(G^{-1})^{cj}\right)\left(a^{-2}(G^{-1})^{dk}\right)a^{3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]_{ijk}=a^{-1}{\varepsilon[G]_{i}}^{\sharp cd}\,.

∎

11.2.
Commutators

Herein, we collect a variety of commutators of spatial derivative operators with each other as well as with time derivatives. While these mostly follow by standard computations, we use the fact that our spatial hypersurfaces are three-dimensional to significantly simplify the spatial commutator formulas, and need to apply the rescaled equations from Proposition
2.10
for the time derivative formulas.

For higher order commutators, we denote by
\mathfrak{J}
terms within the commutator formula that contribute junk terms at any point where this commutator formula is used. Furthermore, in the following,
\zeta
denotes a scalar function on
\overline{M}
and
\mathfrak{T}
denotes a
\Sigma_{t}
-tangent, symmetric
(0,2)
-tensor, always with sufficient regularity for the equations to make sense. Moreover, recall the schematic
\ast
-notation as introduced in subsection
2.1.8
.

Corollary 11.4

(Schematic first order spatial commutators)
.

For
\zeta
and
\mathfrak{T}
as above, the following identities hold:

(11.6a)

\displaystyle[\Delta,\nabla]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\nabla\zeta\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.6b)

\displaystyle[\Delta,\nabla^{2}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\zeta+\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\zeta

(11.6c)

\displaystyle[\Delta,\nabla]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}

(11.6d)

\displaystyle[\Delta,\nabla^{2}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\nabla^{2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}

(11.6e)

\displaystyle[\Delta,\text{{div}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}

(11.6f)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\Delta,\text{{curl}}_{G}]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left(\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}\right)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

Proof.

Since we are working in three spatial dimensions, the following identity holds:

\displaystyle\text{{Riem}}[G]_{ijkl}=

\displaystyle\,G_{ik}\text{{Ric}}[G]_{jl}-G_{il}\text{{Ric}}[G]_{jk}+G_{jl}\text{{Ric}}[G]_{ik}-G_{jk}\text{{Ric}}[G]_{il}

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}(G^{-1})^{mn}\text{{Ric}}[G]_{mn}(G_{ik}G_{jl}-G_{il}G_{jk})

Hence, for any
I\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, any
\nabla^{I}\text{{Riem}}[G]
-term reduces to a sum of products and contractions of
\nabla^{I}\text{{Ric}}[G]
with various metric tensors that are all surpressed in schematic notation. With this in mind, the above statements are simply direct consequences of standard commutation cormulas and (
11.5
).
∎

Lemma 11.5

(Higher order spatial commutators)
.

For
l\in\mathbb{N},\,l\geq 2
, the following formulas hold (and extend to
l=1
when dropping any term involving
\Delta^{l-2}
):

(11.7a)

\displaystyle[\Delta^{l},\nabla]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\zeta+\nabla\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\zeta+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla]\zeta)

(11.7b)

\displaystyle[\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\nabla\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\zeta+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\zeta+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\zeta)

(11.7c)

\displaystyle[\Delta^{l},\nabla]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\nabla\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla]\mathfrak{T})\,,

(11.7d)

\displaystyle[\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}+\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\mathfrak{T})\,,

(11.7e)

\displaystyle[\Delta^{l},\text{{div}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,\nabla\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\text{{div}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T})\,,

(11.7f)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\Delta^{l},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\,\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left(\nabla\Delta^{l-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}\right)+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

with junk terms

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla]\zeta)=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\zeta}=2(l-1),\\
\,I_{\zeta}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+I_{\zeta}=2m}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\zeta)=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\zeta}=2(l-1)+1,\\
I_{1},I_{\zeta}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+I_{\zeta}=2m+1}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla]\mathfrak{T})=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2(l-1)+1,\\
I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2m+1}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\nabla^{2}]\mathfrak{T})=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2l,\\
I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2m+2}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}T

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\text{{div}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T})=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2(l-1)+1,\\
I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2m+1}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{l},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\mathfrak{T})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}=

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left[\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2(l-1)+1,\\
I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}+\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{1}+\dots+I_{l-m}+\\
+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2m+1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}\right]\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

Proof.

The formulas follow by applying the formulas from Lemma
11.4
inductively.
∎

Lemma 11.6

(Time derivative commutators)
.

With respect to a solution to the Einstein scalar field system as in Proposition
2.6
, the following commutator formulas hold:

(11.8a)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla_{i}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,0

(11.8b)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla^{\sharp i}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,2(N+1)a^{-3}\Sigma^{\sharp ij}\nabla_{j}\zeta-2N\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla^{\sharp i}\zeta

(11.8c)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\Delta]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,2(N+1)a^{-3}\langle\Sigma,\nabla^{2}\zeta\rangle_{G}-2N\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta\zeta

\displaystyle\,-2(N+1)a^{-3}\langle\text{{div}}_{G}\Sigma,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}-2a^{-3}\langle\Sigma,\nabla N\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\langle\nabla N,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}

(11.8d)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla_{i}]\mathfrak{T}_{kl}=

\displaystyle\,\left\{2(N+1)a^{-3}\left[\nabla_{i}\Sigma_{(k}^{m}+\nabla_{(k}\Sigma_{i}^{\sharp m}-\nabla^{m}\Sigma_{i(k}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\ +2a^{-3}\left.\left[\nabla_{i}N\Sigma^{\sharp m}_{(k}+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Sigma^{\sharp m}_{i}\nabla_{(k}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\nabla^{\sharp m}N\Sigma_{i(k}\right]\right\}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathfrak{T}_{l)m}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle-\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\left[\nabla_{i}N\mathfrak{T}_{kl}+\nabla_{(k}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathfrak{T}_{l)i}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-\nabla^{\sharp m}NG_{i(k}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathfrak{T}_{l)m}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\right]

(11.8e)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\Delta]\mathfrak{T}_{kl}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\mathfrak{T}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Delta\mathfrak{T}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}\nabla((N+1)\Sigma)\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla N\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\nabla^{2}((N+1)\Sigma)\ast\mathfrak{T}-\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla^{2}N\ast\mathfrak{T}

Proof.

(
11.8a
) is simply that coordinate derivatives commute, and (
11.8b
) follows by applying (
2.28b
) and the product rule.

For the commutators (
11.8c
), (
11.8d
) and (
11.8e
), we write out the covariant derivatives in local coordinates, apply the product rule, and then the evolution equations (
2.28b
) and (
2.34
) for the inverse metric and Christoffel symbols. ∎

Lemma 11.7

(High order time derivative commutators)
.

For
l\in\mathbb{N},\,l\geq 2
, the time derivative commutators take the following form:

(11.9a)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,2a^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Sigma,\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-1}\zeta\rangle_{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-2}\zeta\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\,-2(N+1)a^{-3}\langle\text{{div}}_{G}\Delta^{l-1}\Sigma,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}+(N+1)a^{-3}\nabla^{2l-3}\text{{Ric}}\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\zeta+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}]\zeta),\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.9b)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,2a^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Sigma,\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-1}\zeta\rangle_{G}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2l}\zeta

\displaystyle\,-2(N+1)a^{-3}\langle\nabla\text{{div}}_{G}\Delta^{l-1}\Sigma,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\langle\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-1}N,\nabla\zeta\rangle_{G}+(N+1)a^{-3}\nabla^{2l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\zeta

\displaystyle\,+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\zeta)

(11.9c)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\left(\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-1}\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-2}\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\mathfrak{T}\ast\nabla\Delta^{l-1}\Sigma+\mathfrak{T}\ast\Delta^{l}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\left((N+1)\Sigma\ast\mathfrak{T}\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\nabla((N+1)\Sigma\ast\mathfrak{T})\ast\nabla^{2l-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]\right)

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta^{l}N\cdot\mathfrak{T}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Delta^{l-1}N\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}])\mathfrak{T}\,,

(11.9d)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\nabla\Sigma\ast\Delta^{l}\mathfrak{T}+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-1}\mathfrak{T}+a^{-3}(N+1)\mathfrak{T}\ast\nabla\Delta^{l}\Sigma

\displaystyle+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Delta^{l}N\ast\mathfrak{T}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla^{2}\Delta^{l-1}N\ast\nabla\mathfrak{T}+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{l-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\,+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\mathfrak{T})\,,

where the junk terms take are as follows:

(11.9e)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}]\zeta)=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}=2(l-1)\\
I_{\zeta}\leq 2(l-2)\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+2}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\zeta}=2l\\
I_{\zeta}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}\\
+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}=2m\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+2}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}\\
+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}=2m\\
I_{1}\neq 2l-4\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{m=0}^{l-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}+I_{\zeta}=2m-1\\
\,I_{\zeta}\neq 2(l-1)\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

(11.9f)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\zeta)=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\zeta}=2l\\
\,I_{\zeta}\neq 0\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta+a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}=2(l-1)+1\\
(I_{\Sigma},I_{\zeta})\neq(0,2(l-1)+1),(1,2(l-1))\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}\\
+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}=2m+1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+2}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{l-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}\\
+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}=2m+1\\
I_{1}\neq 2l-3\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{m=0}^{l-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}+I_{\zeta}=2m\\
\,I_{\zeta}\neq 2(l-1)\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

(11.9g)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}])\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2l}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}+a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{\Sigma}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2l\\
I_{\Sigma},I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{l-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}\\
+\sum_{i=1}^{l-m-1}I_{i}=2m\\
I_{1}<2l-3\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{l-m-1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=2l,\,I_{\mathfrak{T}}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

(11.9h)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{l}]\mathfrak{T})=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\Sigma\ast\nabla N\ast\Delta^{l}\text{{Ric}}[G]+a^{-3}N\ast\nabla\Sigma\ast\Delta^{l}\mathfrak{T}+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla N\ast\Delta^{l}\mathfrak{T}+\nabla\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{l}]\mathfrak{T})

We can extend the formulas to
l=1
by dropping any term which would contain negative powers of
\Delta
or a multiindex of negative order.

Proof.

This follows by iteratively applying the commutators in Lemma
11.6
.
∎

While all of the above commutators will be essential for the mainline argument, the a priori estimates require the following commutators:

Lemma 11.8

(Auxiliary commutators)
.

Let
J\in\mathbb{N}
. Then, we have:

(11.10a)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla^{J}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\zeta}=l-1,\,I_{\zeta}<J-1}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\zeta}=J-1,I_{N}>0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

(11.10b)

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla^{J}]\mathfrak{T}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=J,\,I_{\mathfrak{T}}<J}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\mathfrak{T}}=J,I_{N}>0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\mathfrak{T}}}\mathfrak{T}

Proof.

For
J=1
, this has already been shown in (
11.8a
) and (
11.8d
). For higher orders, the formulas follow from a straightforward induction argument using that, in local coordinates, we schematically have

\displaystyle[\partial_{t},\nabla^{J}]\zeta=[\partial_{t},\nabla]\nabla^{J-1}\zeta+\nabla[\partial_{t},\nabla^{J-1}]\zeta=(\partial_{t}\Gamma[G])\ast\nabla^{J-1}\zeta+\nabla[\partial_{t},\nabla^{J-1}]\zeta

and analogously replacing
\zeta
with
\mathfrak{T}
.
∎

11.3.
Borderline and junk terms

Definition 11.9

(Error terms)
.

Let
L\in 2\mathbb{N},\,L\geq 2
. Then, the error terms in the Laplace commuted equations stated in Lemma
2.11
take the following form:

For the constraint equations, we have

(11.11a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{M}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,-8\pi(\Psi+C)\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\phi+\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\Sigma+\underbrace{\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\Sigma}_{\text{if }L\neq 2}

\displaystyle\,+\sum_{I_{\Psi}+I_{\phi}=L,\,I_{\Psi}\neq 0}\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi\ast\nabla^{I_{\phi}+1}\phi+8\pi(\Psi+C)\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]\phi)-\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\text{{div}}_{G}]\Sigma)

(11.11b)

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\tilde{\mathfrak{M}}_{L,Junk}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\,\underbrace{-\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\Sigma}_{\text{if }L\neq 2}-\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\Sigma)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.11c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{H}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-4}\left[\Sigma\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma\right]

(11.11d)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{H}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{I_{1}+I_{2}=L}\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}+1}\phi+a^{-4}\sum_{I_{1}+I_{2}=L,I_{i}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{1}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left[\frac{4\pi}{3}\lvert\nabla\phi\rvert_{G}^{2}+\frac{8\pi}{3}a^{-4}\Psi^{2}+\frac{16\pi}{3}Ca^{-4}\Psi\right]\cdot G

The lapse equation error terms are

(11.12a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{N}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-4}(N+1)\left(\Sigma\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma+\Psi\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi+\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Psi\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-4}\left[\lvert\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}+\Psi^{2}+\Psi\right]\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N+a^{-4}\nabla\left[\lvert\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}+\Psi^{2}+\Psi\right]\ast\nabla^{L-1}N\,

(11.12b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{N}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L;\\
I_{N}\leq L-2;\,I_{N}>0\text{ or }I_{1}\leq I_{2}\leq L-2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\left(\nabla^{I_{1}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Sigma+\nabla^{I_{1}}\Psi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Psi\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-4}N\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi+a^{-4}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L;\,I_{\Psi}\geq 2,\,I_{N}\geq 1}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi\,,

as well as

(11.12c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{N}_{L+1,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-4}(N+1)\Bigr(\Sigma\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L}\Sigma+\nabla^{2}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma+\Psi\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi

\displaystyle\,+\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla^{L}\Psi+\nabla^{2}\Psi\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Psi\Bigr)+a^{-4}\left[\lvert\Sigma\rvert_{G}^{2}+\Psi^{2}+\Psi\right]\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N

\displaystyle\,+\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla^{L}N+\nabla^{2}\Psi\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Psi\,

(11.12d)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{N}_{L+1,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L;\\
I_{N}<L+1;\,I_{N}>0\text{ or }I_{1}\geq I_{2}>2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\left(\nabla^{I_{1}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Sigma+\nabla^{I_{1}}\Psi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Psi\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-4}N\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi+a^{-4}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L+1;\,I_{N},I_{\Psi}>2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi

whereas the scalar field error terms read

(11.13a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,-3\Psi\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla^{L-1}N+2a^{-3}(N+1)\langle\Sigma,\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Psi\rangle_{G}+2a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\Psi

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-2a^{-3}(N+1)\langle\text{{div}}_{G}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma,\nabla\Psi\rangle_{G}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\Psi\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.13b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L,\,I_{\Psi}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi+a\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\phi}=L+1,\,I_{N},I_{\phi}\neq 0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\phi}+1}\phi+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\Psi)

(11.13c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\Psi\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\phi\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{L}\phi\ast\nabla\Sigma\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.13d)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L+1,\,I_{N},I_{\Psi}\neq 0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi+a^{-3}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\ast\nabla^{2}\phi\ast\Sigma

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+(N+1)a^{-3}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\left((N+1)\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi\right)+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\langle\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N,\nabla\phi\rangle_{G}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\phi)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

and

(11.13e)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{L+1,Border}=

\displaystyle\,-3\Psi\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N+2a^{-3}\langle\Sigma,\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Psi\rangle_{G}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L}\Psi+2a^{-3}\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\Psi

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma\ast\nabla\Psi\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.13f)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{P}_{L+1,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L+1,\,I_{\Psi}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi+a\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\phi}=L+2,\,I_{N},I_{\phi}\neq 0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\phi}+1}\phi

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\Psi)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.13g)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{L+1,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\Psi\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}+1}N+a^{-3}\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\ast\nabla\phi

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\phi\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{L+1,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Psi}=L+2,\,2\leq I_{\Psi}\leq L+1}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}\Psi+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi

(11.13h)

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}+1}]\phi)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

as well as

(11.13i)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{1,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\Psi\Delta N+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\phi

(11.13j)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{Q}_{1,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\nabla\Psi\ast\nabla N\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+a^{-3}(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla\phi+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta]\phi)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\,.

The commuted rescaled evolution equation for
\Sigma
has the error terms

(11.14a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{S}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}(N+1)\left(\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\Sigma\right)+a^{-3}\left(\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot(\Sigma\ast\Sigma)+\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\ast\nabla\Sigma\ast\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}(N+1)\Sigma\ast\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\ast\Sigma+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\ast\nabla\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{a^{-3}[(N+1)\nabla\Sigma\ast\Sigma+\nabla N\ast\Sigma\ast\Sigma]\ast\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.14b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{S}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,-a[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla^{2}]N+a\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\text{{Ric}}}=L,I_{N}\neq 0}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\text{{Ric}}}}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}=L}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{I_{1}+I_{2}=L,\,I_{i}>0}\nabla^{I_{1}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Sigma+a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L,\,I_{N}<L}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+a\sum_{I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}+1}\phi+\left(4\pi C^{2}a^{-3}+\frac{1}{3}a\right)\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot G+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\Sigma)

while the commuted Ricci tensor evolution equations have error terms

(11.15a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\left[\nabla^{L+2}N\cdot\Sigma+\nabla^{L+1}N\ast\nabla\Sigma+\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\right]

(11.15b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{L+1,Border}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\left[\nabla^{L+3}N\cdot\Sigma+\nabla^{L+2}N\ast\nabla\Sigma+\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{L}\text{{Ric}}[G]\right]

(11.15c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}=L+2,\,I_{\Sigma}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle+a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\text{{Ric}}}=L\\
(I_{\Sigma},I_{\text{{Ric}}})\neq(0,L),(1,L-1)\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\text{{Ric}}}}\text{{Ric}}[G]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\sum_{{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+\sum_{i=1}^{\nicefrac{{L}}{{2}}-m+1}I_{i}=2m}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\ast\nabla^{I_{\frac{L}{2}-m+1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\left([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla^{2}]N+\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\ast\text{{Ric}}[G]+\nabla^{L-1}N\ast\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\right)+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\text{{Ric}}[G])

(11.15d)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{R}_{L+1,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}=L+3,\,I_{\Sigma}\geq 2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle+a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{\text{{Ric}}}=L\\
(I_{\Sigma},I_{\text{{Ric}}})\neq(0,L+1),(1,L)\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{\text{{Ric}}}}\text{{Ric}}[G]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{m=0}^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\sum_{{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+\sum_{i=1}^{\nicefrac{{L}}{{2}}-m+1}I_{i}=2m+1}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\dots\nabla^{I_{\frac{L}{2}-m+1}}\text{{Ric}}[G]

\displaystyle\,+\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\left(\nabla[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla^{2}]N+\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\ast\text{{Ric}}[G]+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\ast\nabla\text{{Ric}}[G]\right)+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\text{{Ric}}[G])

Finally, the Bel-Robinson evolution error terms are

(11.16a)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\tau}{3}\left(\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\bm{E}+\nabla^{L-1}N\ast\nabla\bm{E}\right)-a^{-1}\left(\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\bm{E}\times\Sigma+\bm{E}\times\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left(\nabla^{L-1}\bm{E}\ast\nabla\Sigma+\nabla\bm{E}\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot(\bm{E}\ast\Sigma)+a^{-3}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\ast[\nabla\bm{E}\ast\Sigma+\bm{E}\ast\nabla\Sigma]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\left(\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\bm{E}+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\bm{E}+\nabla\bm{E}\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma+\bm{E}\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\left[(N+1)\Sigma\ast\bm{E}\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\underbrace{\nabla\left((N+1)\ast\Sigma\ast\bm{E}\right)\ast\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]}_{\text{if }L\neq 2}\right]

\displaystyle\,+4\pi a^{-3}(\Psi+C)^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\Sigma\,+4\pi a^{-3}\nabla^{L-1}N\ast\left[(\Psi+C)^{2}\nabla\Sigma+2(\Psi+C)\ast\nabla\Psi\ast\Sigma\right]

\displaystyle\,+4\pi a^{-3}(N+1)\left[(\Psi^{2}+2C\Psi)\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma+2(\Psi+C)\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Psi\cdot\Sigma\right]

\displaystyle\,+4\pi a^{-3}\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma\ast\left[(\Psi+C)^{2}\nabla N+2(N+1)(\Psi+C)\nabla\Psi\right]

\displaystyle\,+4\pi a^{-3}(\Psi+C)\nabla^{L-1}\Psi\ast\left[(N+1)\nabla\Sigma+\nabla N\ast\Sigma\right]

(11.16b)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{L,top}=

\displaystyle\,a^{-1}(N+1)\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{B}\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]+a(N+1)(\Psi+C)\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\phi

(11.16c)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{E}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\bm{E}}=L,\,I_{N}\leq L-2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{E}}}\bm{E}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left[\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\bm{B}}=L+1,\,I_{N},I_{\bm{B}}\leq L}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{B}}}\bm{B}+(N+1)\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\bm{B}\right]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\bm{E}}+I_{\Sigma}=L,\\
I_{N}\leq L-2;\,I_{N}>0\text{ or }I_{\bm{E}},I_{\Sigma}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{E}}}\bm{E}\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+a\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Psi}+I_{\phi}=L+1\\
I_{N},I_{\Psi},I_{\phi}\neq L+1\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}(\Psi+C)\ast\nabla^{I_{\phi}+1}\phi

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L\\
I_{N},I_{\Sigma},I_{i}\leq L-2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}}(\Psi+C)\ast\nabla^{I_{2}}(\Psi+C)

\displaystyle\,+\dot{a}a^{3}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}+1}\phi

\displaystyle\,+a\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\Sigma}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}+1}\phi

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\varepsilon[G]\ast\bm{B}\ast[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]N+4\pi a(N+1)(\Psi+C)\left[\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\phi+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla^{2}]\phi)\right]

\displaystyle\,+a\left\{(\Psi+C)[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]N+(N+1)[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]\Psi\right\}\ast\nabla\phi+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}(N+1)a^{-1}\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\bm{B})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\bm{E})

\displaystyle\,+\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left[a^{-3}(N+1)\bm{E}\ast\Sigma+\frac{2\pi}{3}a^{6}(N+1)\left(\partial_{0}\left(a^{-6}(\Psi+C)^{2}+a^{-2}\lvert\nabla\phi\rvert_{G}^{2}\right)+4\pi\frac{\dot{a}}{a}(\Psi+C)^{2}\right)\right]\cdot G

(11.16d)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}_{L,Border}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\tau}{3}\left(\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot\bm{B}+\nabla^{L-1}N\ast\nabla\bm{B}\right)-a^{-1}\left(\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\bm{B}\times\Sigma+\bm{B}\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left(\nabla^{L-1}\bm{B}\ast\nabla\Sigma+\nabla\bm{B}\ast\nabla^{L-1}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}N\cdot(\bm{B}\ast\Sigma)+a^{-3}\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}N\cdot\left[\nabla\bm{B}\ast\Sigma+\bm{B}\ast\nabla\Sigma\right]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\left(\Sigma\ast\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\bm{B}+\nabla\Sigma\ast\nabla^{3}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\bm{B}+\nabla\bm{B}\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\Sigma+\bm{B}\ast\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\Sigma\right)

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\left[(N+1)\Sigma\ast\bm{B}\ast\nabla^{L-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]+\underbrace{\nabla\left((N+1)\ast\Sigma\ast\bm{B}\right)\ast\nabla^{L-3}\text{{Ric}}[G]}_{\text{if }L\neq 2}\right]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}(N+1)(\Psi+C)\cdot\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\phi\ast\Sigma+a^{-1}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\nabla^{L}\phi\ast\nabla\left((N+1)(\Psi+C)\Sigma\right)

(11.16e)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}_{L,top}=

\displaystyle\,a^{3}(N+1)\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\phi\ast\nabla\phi+a^{-1}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{E}\ast\nabla\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-1}\text{{Ric}}[G]

(11.16f)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{B}_{L,Junk}=

\displaystyle\,\frac{\dot{a}}{a}\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\bm{B}}=L,I_{N}\leq L-2}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{B}}}\bm{B}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\left[\sum_{I_{N}+I_{\bm{E}}=L+1,I_{N},I_{\bm{E}}\leq L}\nabla^{I_{N}}N\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{E}}}\bm{E}+\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla\bm{E}\right]

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\bm{E}}+I_{\Sigma}=L,\\
I_{N}\leq L-2;\,I_{N}>0\text{ or }I_{\bm{B}},I_{\Sigma}\geq 2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\bm{B}}}\bm{B}\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+a^{3}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{1}+I_{2}=L,\\
\,I_{N}>0\text{ or }I_{2}<L\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{1}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{2}+2}\phi

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{N}+I_{\Psi}+I_{\phi}+I_{\Sigma}=L\\
I_{\phi}\leq L-2\end{subarray}}\nabla^{I_{N}}(N+1)\ast\nabla^{I_{\Psi}}(\Psi+C)\ast\nabla^{I_{\phi}+1}\phi\ast\nabla^{I_{\Sigma}}\Sigma

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}\varepsilon[G]\ast\bm{E}\ast[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]N

\displaystyle\,+a^{-1}(N+1)(\Psi+C)\cdot\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\Sigma\ast\left[\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla\right]\phi

\displaystyle\,+a^{3}(N+1)\bm{\varepsilon}[G]\ast\nabla\phi\ast\left(\nabla^{2}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}-2}\text{{Ric}}[G]\ast\nabla^{2}\phi+\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\nabla]\phi)\right)

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-a^{-1}(N+1)\mathfrak{J}([\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}},\text{{curl}}_{G}]\bm{E})\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\mathfrak{J}([\partial_{t},\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}]\bm{B})+\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left[a^{-3}(N+1)\bm{B}\ast\Sigma\right]\cdot G

\displaystyle\,+\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\left[4\pi a^{2}\nabla^{\sharp m}\phi(\Psi+C)+\frac{2\pi}{3}a^{5}\Delta^{\frac{L}{2}}\nabla^{\sharp m}\left(a^{-6}(\Psi+C)^{2}+a^{-2}\lvert\nabla\phi\rvert_{G}^{2}\right)\right]\bm{\varepsilon}[G]_{(\cdot)m(\cdot)}

11.4.
L^{2}_{G}
error term estimates

In this subsection, we collect how the error terms can be controlled in terms of energies as well as homogenous Sobolev norms of
\phi
. We don’t claim that these estimates are optimal – in particular, we note that at low order (like
L=2
), many of the curvature errors that appear in the estimates below could be avoided entirely: These arise as a result of applying the general estimates in Lemma
4.5
where the Ricci tensor doesn’t naturally occur in the respective equations, and can be avoided at low orders by applying (
4.4f
) on all curvature terms that occur.

Instead of optimality, we try to keep both notation and form of the error term estimates as simple as possible and the energy estimates between base and top level as unified as possible. In particular, we track the
“worst”
curvature energy occurring at high orders for all estimates below, even if these terms are added in artificially for low orders.

Lemma 11.10

(Estimates for borderline error terms)
.

Let
L\in 2\mathbb{Z}_{+},\,L\leq 20
. Then, the following estimates hold:

(11.17a)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{H}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-4}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.17b)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{N}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-4}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right]+\varepsilon a^{-4}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{-4}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.17c)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{N}_{L+1,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-6}\left[\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right]+\varepsilon a^{-6}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-4}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.17d)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{P}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-3)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.17e)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{Q}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}

(11.17f)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{P}_{L+1,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.17g)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{Q}_{L+1,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+2)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon\color[rgb]{0,0,0}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)}

(11.17h)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{S}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.17i)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+2)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.17j)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}_{L+1,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+3)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.17k)

\displaystyle\,\|\mathfrak{E}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}+\|\mathfrak{B}_{L,Border}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right)+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(W,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(W,\cdot)}\right)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

Proof.

All of these estimates follow from applying
L^{2}_{G}
-
L^{\infty}_{G}
-type Hölder estimates to the individual nonlinear terms. The lower order terms are either controlled by the zero order estimates in subsection
4.1
or the a priori estimates in Lemma
4.3
. Furthermore, we apply Lemma
4.5
, along with again Lemma
4.3
, to translate
L^{2}_{G}
-norms into energies up to additional curvature energy terms. For the sake of simplicity, we always estimate
\frac{\dot{a}}{a}
by
a^{-3}
up to constant (see (
2.3
)), and liberally apply (
3.8
) to deal with odd order energies and to distribute
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
factors to lower orders while updating
c>0
whereever this is convenient.
∎

Lemma 11.11

(Estimates for top order error terms)
.

(11.18a)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{E}_{L,top}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}=\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.18b)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}_{L,top}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-1}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}=\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

Proof.

This follows directly using (
4.2c
) and (
4.4g
) for the Bel-Robinson terms as well as (
4.4e
).
∎

Lemma 11.12

(Junk terms)
.

Recalling
\parallel
-notation from Remark
2.12
, the following holds:

(11.19a)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{M}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.19b)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathfrak{M}}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

(11.19c)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{H}_{L,Junk}^{\parallel}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-2-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.19d)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{N}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon a^{-4}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.19e)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{N}_{L+1,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-c\sigma}\left(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right)

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\left[\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\right]

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-4}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{-4-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-3)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.19f)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{P}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\left[\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right]\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-3)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.19g)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{P}_{L+1,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\left[\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right]\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{a^{4}\mathcal{E}^{(L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\left(\varepsilon^{2}a^{-1-c\sigma}+\varepsilon^{2}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.19h)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{Q}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\,+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.19i)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{Q}_{1,Junk}\|\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathcal{E}^{(1)}(N,\cdot)+\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\|\nabla\phi\|_{L^{2}_{G}}+\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq 1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(11.19j)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{Q}_{L+1,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{a^{-4}\mathcal{E}^{(L+1)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\phi,\cdot)\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

\displaystyle\,\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

(11.19k)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{S}_{L,Junk}^{\parallel}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\left(\varepsilon a^{-3}+a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{5-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\underbrace{\varepsilon a^{-3}\mathcal{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

(11.19l)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}_{L,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}

(11.19m)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{R}_{L+1,Junk}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon^{2}a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-1)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+3)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+1)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L+1)}(N,\cdot)}

(11.19n)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{E}_{L,Junk}^{\parallel}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}+\|\mathfrak{B}_{L,Junk}^{\parallel}\|_{L^{2}_{G}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\,\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(W,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(W,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\phi,\cdot)}+\left(\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}+a^{-1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\right)\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\phi,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\sqrt{\varepsilon}a^{1-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L)}(N,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(N,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-1-c\sigma}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-2)}(\Sigma,\cdot)}

\displaystyle\,+\varepsilon a^{-3}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(L-2)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}+\varepsilon a^{-3-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}\underbrace{\sqrt{\mathcal{E}^{(\leq L-4)}(\text{{Ric}},\cdot)}}_{\text{not present for }L=2}

Proof.

Once again, this follows by applying the a priori estimates from subsection
4.1
and Lemma
4.3
, as well as the bootstrap assumption (
3.17h
) for the lapse, to deal with the lower order terms in the nonlinearities, and then applying Lemma
4.5
as well as (
3.8
) whereever this is necessary. Further, especially in (
11.19n
), it is often more convenient to use the bootstrap assumption for
\|\nabla\phi\|_{C_{G}}
instead of the a priori estimate (
4.4e
) to gain higher powers of
\varepsilon
in prefactors.

Recognizing that every low order curvature term can be estimated up to constant by
a^{-c\sqrt{\varepsilon}}
at worst (see (
4.4f
)), we also note that any of the highly nonlinear curvature terms in
\mathfrak{J}
-expressions turn out to be negligible after updating
c
compared to Ricci energies arising from applying Lemma
4.5
or compared to junk terms in which
\text{{Ric}}[G]
is tracked explicitly. ∎

12.
Appendix – Future stability

Here, we collect the commutators in CMCSH gauge necessary to study the commuted scalar-field equations:

Lemma 12.1

(Commutator formulas for future stabilty)
.

Let
\zeta
be a scalar function on
\Sigma_{T}
. Then, the following formulas hold:

\displaystyle[\widetilde{\partial_{0}},\nabla]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,0

\displaystyle[\widetilde{\partial_{0}},\Delta_{\bm{g}}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,(\widetilde{\partial_{0}}(\bm{g}^{-1})^{ab})\nabla_{a}\nabla_{b}\zeta-2(\bm{g}^{-1})^{ab}\left(\text{{div}}_{\bm{g}}(\bm{n}\bm{\Sigma})_{a}-2\nabla_{a}\bm{n}\right)\nabla_{b}\zeta

Schematically, for
k\in\mathbb{N}
, this implies

(12.1a)

\displaystyle[\widetilde{\partial_{0}},\Delta_{\bm{g}}^{k}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{I_{\bm{n}}+I_{\bm{\Sigma}}+I_{\zeta}=2k-1}\nabla^{I_{\bm{n}}}\bm{n}\ast_{\bm{g}}\nabla^{I_{\bm{\Sigma}}}\bm{\Sigma}\ast_{\bm{g}}\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

(12.1b)

\displaystyle[\widetilde{\partial_{0}},\nabla\Delta_{\bm{g}}^{k}]\zeta=

\displaystyle\,\sum_{I_{\bm{n}}+I_{\bm{\Sigma}}+I_{\zeta}=2k}\nabla^{I_{\bm{n}}}\bm{n}\ast_{\bm{g}}\nabla^{I_{\bm{\Sigma}}}\bm{\Sigma}\ast_{\bm{g}}\nabla^{I_{\zeta}+1}\zeta

Proof.

This follows from straightfoward computations, similar to Lemma
11.6
for the low order commutators and to Lemma
11.7
for higher orders.
∎
